## `AFG-1812-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Melt-Adhesion Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Post-Press Book Folding & Hot-Melt Spine Adhesion Tensile
- **Intro:** Melt-Adhesion Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Post-Press Book Folding & Hot-Melt Spine Adhesion Tensile — Pre-Start Safety and PPE Verification: PPE must be free of tears or contamination. Emergency stop buttons must be unobstructed and functional.
- **Key tolerance:** PPE must be free of tears or contamination. Emergency stop buttons must be unobstructed and functional.
- **Standards:** `ISO 9001:2015`, `ISO 11612:2015`, `ANSI/ISEA 105-2016`, `IEC 60204-1:2016`

## `AFG-1812-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Melt-Adhesion Inspector
- **Scenario:** Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Post-Press Book Folding & Hot-Melt Spine Adhesion Tensile
- **Intro:** Melt-Adhesion Inspector — Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Post-Press Book Folding & Hot-Melt Spine Adhesion Tensile — Verify Batch Documentation and Material Certificates: Paper grammage: 80 g/m² ± 2 g/m²; Adhesive MFI: 25 g/10 min ± 3 g/10 min (at 190°C, 2.16 kg).
- **Key tolerance:** Paper grammage: 80 g/m² ± 2 g/m²; Adhesive MFI: 25 g/10 min ± 3 g/10 min (at 190°C, 2.16 kg).
- **Standards:** `ISO 9001:2015`, `ISO 11607-1:2006`, `ASTM D1876-08 Standard Test Method for Peel Resistance of Adhesi`, `ANSI/ASQ Z1.4-2008 Sampling Procedures and Tables for Inspection`

- **Role:** Guillotine-Guard Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** 4M1E Engineering Change (ECN) Containment Approval & 8D/CAPA Root Cause Rectification SOP for Pre-Press Chemical Waste Neutralization & Guillotine Cutter Safety Monitor Incidents

## `AGO-1812-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Binding-Line Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Computer-to-Plate Laser Imaging & Chemical Developing
